<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48865019" data-id="48865019" data-tags="Legal|AI" data-score="1612" data-comments="920" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019" data-time="1783716429">
<a class="thumb" href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/10/apple-sues-openai-trade-secret-theft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/05/apple-openai.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='9to5mac.com' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/10/apple-sues-openai-trade-secret-theft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple sues OpenAI, accuses ex-employees of stealing trade secrets</span><span class='zh'>苹果控告 OpenAI，指控前员工窃取商业机密</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff.</span><span class='zh'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff. The case centers on proprietary AI techniques and internal data siphoned during a transition, signaling growing tension as big tech fights over AI talent. The outcome could influence how firms protect confidential knowledge during acquisitions and departures.</span><span class='zh'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。此案凸显大型科技公司在人才流动与知识保护方面的博弈，将影响企业在并购和员工离职时的机密信息防护策略。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1612</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>920</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48843923" data-id="48843923" data-tags="Politics|Privacy|Security" data-score="1603" data-comments="836" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843923" data-time="1783595034">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/eu-parliament-greenlights-chat-control-1-0-breyer-our-children-lose-out/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f97316' data-tag='Politics'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=patrick-breyer.de&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>patrick-breyer.de</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/eu-parliament-greenlights-chat-control-1-0-breyer-our-children-lose-out/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU Parliament greenlights Chat Control 1.0</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会通过聊天监控1.0，Breyer：我们的孩子要吃亏</span></a><span class="dom">patrick-breyer.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention.</span><span class='zh'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention. Critics warn that the measure expands surveillance at the expense of privacy and free expression, while Breyer argues that children will lose out.</span><span class='zh'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。批评者担心此举侵蚀隐私与言论自由，而Breyer则认为这会让儿童吃亏。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/gpt-5-6/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 版本</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new GPT-5.6 model appears, signaling continued iteration on the GPT-5 family.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 版正逐步进入，表示 GPT-5 系列的持续迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new GPT-5.6 model appears, signaling continued iteration on the GPT-5 family. Expect refinements in reasoning, safety, and efficiency, reflecting the ongoing cadence of large language model updates.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 版正逐步进入，表示 GPT-5 系列的持续迭代。预计在推理、安全与效率方面有改进，体现大语言模型更新的持续节奏。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='tris.sherliker.net' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decoding the obfuscated bash script on a Uniqlo t-shirt</span><span class='zh'>解码优衣库 T 恤上的混淆 Bash 脚本</span></a><span class="dom">tris.sherliker.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN. The stunt demonstrates how software-like code can slip into consumer channels via retail distribution, highlighting trust and supply-chain risks. It argues for stronger code provenance checks and verification in modern supply networks.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。这一举动揭示了软件代码通过零售渠道渗透到消费端的风险与供应链信任问题，并呼吁在现代供应链中加强代码来源核验。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apnews.com/article/john-deere-right-to-repair-agriculture-equipment-cb7514ffedb95c130a976af661f2bc02" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John Deere owners will get the right to repair equipment under FTC settlement</span><span class='zh'>FTC和解赋予约翰迪尔设备的修理权</span></a><span class="dom">apnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs.</span><span class='zh'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs. It signals a shift toward repairability by nudging OEMs to share tools, manuals, and parts, and could boost independent repair shops. This case could influence broader right-to-repair momentum in tech-enabled farming.</span><span class='zh'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。此举或促成独立修理商的兴起，或对农业科技领域的修理权运动产生示范效应。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.opentools.studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter：开放打印机项目</span></a><span class="dom">opentools.studio</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers. The initiative could accelerate repairability, transparency, and ecosystem innovation for makers and businesses alike.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。该方向可能促进可修复性、透明度和生态创新，但也需关注可持续性与供应链问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openwrt.org/toh/openwrt/one" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One – Open Hardware Router</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One：开源硬件路由器</span></a><span class="dom">openwrt.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end.</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end. The project signals a move toward verifiably open networking gear that prioritizes user sovereignty, security, and long-term repairability. It could reshape how hobbyists source and build their own routers.</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。该项目预示着向更可验证的开源网络设备转变，强调用户自主性、安全性及长期可维护性。可能改变爱好者自建路由器的选型与实现方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/malisper/pgrust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres rewritten in Rust, now passing 100% of the Postgres regression tests</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL，现已通过 100% 回归测试</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software.</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software. While not a drop-in replacement, the effort demonstrates how safer memory management and modern tooling could influence database internals.</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。虽然不是直接的替代，但这项工作展示了更安全的内存管理与现代工具链对数据库内部实现的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.comaps.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CoMaps – FOSS Offline Maps</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps：开源离线地图</span></a><span class="dom">comaps.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access.</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access. It emphasizes privacy and local control by bundling downloadable map data and routing logic, avoiding reliance on centralized services. This makes it appealing for privacy-minded users and developers who want a portable, maintainable map solution.</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。它强调隐私与本地控制，提供可下载的地图数据与路由逻辑，避免对集中服务的依赖。对注重隐私的用户和开发者而言，这是一种可移植、可维护的地图方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://allaboutcookies.org/eu-mandatory-distracted-driver-system" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every new car sold in the European Union must include a driver monitoring camera</span><span class='zh'>欧盟规定所有新车必须装配驾驶员监控摄像头</span></a><span class="dom">allaboutcookies.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents. The requirement will push automakers to integrate interior cameras and analytics, raising privacy, data handling, and compliance concerns. Given the EU market&#x27;s scale, this could set a de facto global safety standard for vehicle interiors.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。厂商需在车内安装摄像头并接入分析功能，同时引发隐私保护、数据处理与合规性方面的担忧。凭借其市场规模，该举或将为全球车辆内饰安全标准定调。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GPT-Live introduces live, real-time interactions with updated data and external tools, enabling more dynamic conversations.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-Live 引入实时、动态的数据和外部工具交互，使对话具有更即时的更新能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Bun project is exploring a Rust rewrite to address safety and performance limitations of the original runtime. The note discusses why a Rust-based core could improve reliability and tooling integration, while also outlining migration and compatibility challenges that come with rewriting a mature platform.</span><span class='zh'>该文章探讨 Bun 以 Rust 重写核心的原因，强调安全性和性能提升的潜力，以及对可靠性与工具链集成的影响。还提及迁移路线和兼容性挑战等在对成熟平台进行重写时需要权衡的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QuadRF can spot drones and see WiFi through my wall</span><span class='zh'>QuadRF 能侦测无人机并穿墙探测 WiFi 信号</span></a><span class="dom">jeffgeerling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls.</span><span class='zh'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls. This kind of passive observation highlights security and privacy implications for home networks and aerial devices, even when devices aren&#x27;t in visible sight. It also underscores practical limits like range, clutter, and legal boundaries.</span><span class='zh'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。这类被动观测引发对家庭网络和无人机安全与隐私的担忧，同时也暴露出覆盖范围、信号干扰和法律边界等局限。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.xbox.com/en-us/2026/07/06/resetting-xbox/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Resetting Xbox</span><span class='zh'>Xbox 重置</span></a><span class="dom">news.xbox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased.</span><span class='zh'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased. The guidance aims to give players a reliable recovery path for performance issues, software glitches, or corrupted saves. The update underscores the importance of predictable recovery flows as consoles grow more complex.</span><span class='zh'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。该指南为应对性能问题、软件故障或数据损坏提供可靠的恢复路径。随着主机软件日益复杂，可靠的恢复流程显得尤为重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TypeScript 7 brings major improvements to the type system, smarter inference, and faster builds, plus new compiler features that reduce runtime errors.</span><span class='zh'>TypeScript 7 对类型系统、推断能力和构建速度带来重大改进，并引入新的编译器特性以降低运行时错误。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TypeScript 7 brings major improvements to the type system, smarter inference, and faster builds, plus new compiler features that reduce runtime errors. It also updates project configuration and editor tooling.</span><span class='zh'>TypeScript 7 对类型系统、推断能力和构建速度带来重大改进，并引入新的编译器特性以降低运行时错误。此次发布还对项目配置和编辑器工具链进行了更新。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</span><span class='zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</span></a><span class="dom">popcar.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU Parliament gave initial approval to the Chat Control proposal, advancing it to the next legislative stage. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges. The move aims to improve consumer protection and force clearer disclosures from subscription-based services, potentially reshaping how onboarding and renewal terms are presented online. This could raise the bar for startups and platforms relying on subscription models.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。此举将促使订阅型服务在用户注册与续订条款上提供更清晰的披露，或对采用订阅模式的企业带来影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project uses the Fable language to turn a reMarkable tablet into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter, blending code with a literary interface.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的项目利用 Fable 将 reMarkable 平板打造成《哈利波特》里汤姆·里德尔的日记界面，结合编程与叙事界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful project uses the Fable language to turn a reMarkable tablet into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter, blending code with a literary interface. It demonstrates how a programmable notebook can host narrative-driven experiences on niche hardware and invites creative experimentation with data-binding and UI.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的项目利用 Fable 将 reMarkable 平板打造成《哈利波特》里汤姆·里德尔的日记界面，结合编程与叙事界面。它展示了可编程笔记本在小众硬件上实现叙事体验的潜力，并鼓励在数据绑定与用户界面方面进行创意探索。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=systima.ai&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>systima.ai</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">33</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</span></a><span class="dom">systima.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://noma.security/blog/gitlost-how-we-tricked-githubs-ai-agent-into-leaking-private-repos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitLost: We Tricked GitHub&#x27;s AI Agent into Leaking Private Repos</span><span class='zh'>GitLost：我们欺骗 GitHub 的 AI 代理泄露私有仓库</span></a><span class="dom">noma.security</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories. By manipulating prompts and data flows, they demonstrate leakage risks from copilots and automated tooling. This underscores the need for stronger data-handling controls, better prompt safeguards, and auditability in AI-assisted development ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。通过操纵提示和数据流，他们演示了协作工具与自动化流程中的数据泄露风险。这凸显了在 AI 辅助开发生态中加强数据处理、提示防护和审计机制的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/04d1d1e4-bc75-476a-97cf-49055cd98d31/cdc_proof.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra produces proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 产出循环双覆盖猜想的证明（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">cdn.openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided. If verified, this would mark a notable milestone in AI-assisted formal proofs and could accelerate future mathematical work, but the proof will be rigorously scrutinized by the community. The episode highlights AI&#x27;s potential to aid deep mathematics while underscoring the need for careful validation.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。若经同行验证，这一成果可能标志着 AI 辅助形式化证明迈出重要一步，或加速后续的数学研究，但证明将接受严格审查。此事凸显 AI 在高深数学领域的潜力，同时也强调需要谨慎验证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gingerbill.org/article/2026/07/10/good-tools-are-invisible/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Good Tools Are Invisible</span><span class='zh'>好工具往往无形</span></a><span class="dom">gingerbill.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Great tools fade into the background by design, reducing cognitive load and letting people focus on what matters.</span><span class='zh'>优秀的工具被设计为在工作流中隐形，降低认知负担，让人专注于核心任务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://whynothugo.nl/journal/2026/07/03/98-isnt-very-much/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>98% isn&#x27;t much</span><span class='zh'>98% 并不算多</span></a><span class="dom">whynothugo.nl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for.</span><span class='zh'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for. The piece argues for richer metrics and stress testing to avoid overestimating performance in real-world use.</span><span class='zh'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。98% 这样的数字若忽略基线、样本偏差或边缘案例，容易带来错误解读。建议采用更全面的指标和鲁棒性测试来评估真实世界表现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cyberinsider.com/eu-now-one-step-away-from-reviving-private-message-scanning-rules/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU now one step away from reviving private message scanning rules</span><span class='zh'>欧盟只差一步就能重新启用私信扫描规则</span></a><span class="dom">cyberinsider.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU is nearing revival of private-message scanning rules, aiming to mandate messaging platforms to scan private content for illegal activity.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟正在推动重新启用对私信的内容扫描规则，拟要求通讯平台对私人内容进行非法活动的检测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU is nearing revival of private-message scanning rules, aiming to mandate messaging platforms to scan private content for illegal activity. The move would reshape safety vs. privacy dynamics, pressuring platforms to deploy more aggressive monitoring and data processing.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟正在推动重新启用对私信的内容扫描规则，拟要求通讯平台对私人内容进行非法活动的检测。此举将重新定义安全与隐私的平衡，推动更大规模的监控与数据处理，并引发隐私担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/global-workspace" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A global workspace in language models</span><span class='zh'>语言模型的全球工作区</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The paper introduces a global workspace architecture for language-model systems, a shared memory-like substrate that coordinates tools, modules, and reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>该研究提出了语言模型系统的全球工作区架构，作为一个共享的记忆式底层，用于协调工具、模块与推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The paper introduces a global workspace architecture for language-model systems, a shared memory-like substrate that coordinates tools, modules, and reasoning. By enabling components to broadcast and fuse information, it aims to improve long-horizon planning, multi-step reasoning, and robustness in AI agents. This concept could drive more modular, interpretable, and scalable AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>该研究提出了语言模型系统的全球工作区架构，作为一个共享的记忆式底层，用于协调工具、模块与推理。通过让各组件进行信息广播与融合，它旨在提升长程规划、多步推理与系统鲁棒性。此概念有望推动更模块化、可解释且可扩展的 AI 系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://acoup.blog/2026/01/30/collections-the-late-bronze-age-collapse-a-very-brief-introduction/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Late Bronze Age Collapse</span><span class='zh'>晚青铜时代崩溃</span></a><span class="dom">acoup.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Late Bronze Age collapse refers to a complex, multi-state disruption around 1200 BCE.</span><span class='zh'>晚青铜时代崩溃指的是公元前约1200年的多国共同性崩溃，理论包括干旱、饥荒、入侵与贸易网络破裂等。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Late Bronze Age collapse refers to a complex, multi-state disruption around 1200 BCE. The post offers a concise introduction to prevailing theories—from drought and famine to invasions and trade breakdown. It matters because it informs how interconnected civilizations can fail, a topic with resonance for modern supply chains and risk management.</span><span class='zh'>晚青铜时代崩溃指的是公元前约1200年的多国共同性崩溃，理论包括干旱、饥荒、入侵与贸易网络破裂等。该简介帮助理解为何高度互联的文明体系会崩溃，这对当今的供应链与风险管理仍具启示意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.brown.edu/news/2026-07-09/chemical-bonds-relativity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Einstein&#x27;s relativity rules chemical bonds in heavy elements, new research shows</span><span class='zh'>新研究称相对论决定重元素的化学键</span></a><span class="dom">brown.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relativistic effects become significant in very heavy elements, reshaping electron orbitals and bond strengths.</span><span class='zh'>在极重元素中，相对论效应显著改变电子轨道、键能与分子几何。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relativistic effects become significant in very heavy elements, reshaping electron orbitals and bond strengths. The new work shows that Einstein&#x27;s relativity plays a decisive role in how chemical bonds form in superheavy elements, with implications for modeling their chemistry and discovering new materials. This challenges non-relativistic intuition that underpins much of chemistry for lighter elements.</span><span class='zh'>在极重元素中，相对论效应显著改变电子轨道、键能与分子几何。最新研究表明，爱因斯坦的相对论在超重元素的化学键形成中发挥关键作用，这可能改变对其化学性质的建模与新材料的设计。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scrapfly.dev/posts/browser-math-os-fingerprint/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Since Chromium 148, Math.tanh is now fingerprintable to link underlying OS</span><span class='zh'>自 Chromium 148 以来，Math.tanh 可用于指纹识别并关联底层操作系统</span></a><span class="dom">scrapfly.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chromium 148 adds a fingerprintable signal from Math.tanh that can correlate browser observations with host OS characteristics.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 148 引入了 Math.tanh 的指纹信号，能将浏览器观察与底层操作系统特征联系起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chromium 148 adds a fingerprintable signal from Math.tanh that can correlate browser observations with host OS characteristics. In practice, sites could exploit this as a new cross-site fingerprinting channel to tie a user’s browser to their machine, challenging existing privacy protections. The change highlights how tiny API quirks can expand tracking risks and may drive new mitigations or detection techniques.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 148 引入了 Math.tanh 的指纹信号，能将浏览器观察与底层操作系统特征联系起来。简单说，就是一个新的跨站指纹通道，可能被用来把用户的浏览器与其机器绑定，削弱现有的隐私防护。这一小细节再次暴露了浏览器 API 与隐私之间的博弈，或促使厂商和研究者研究对策。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>383</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884853' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>183</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48821848" data-id="48821848" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Privacy" data-score="380" data-comments="98" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821848" data-time="1783449869">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://herdr.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Herdr: One terminal to rule them all</span><span class='zh'>Herdr：一个终端统治所有终端</span></a><span class="dom">herdr.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr bills itself as a universal terminal experience, consolidating multiple shell sessions and workflows into one interface.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 主打“一个终端统治一切”的理念，将多个 shell 会话和工作流集中在同一界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Herdr bills itself as a universal terminal experience, consolidating multiple shell sessions and workflows into one interface. The project targets developers who juggle SSH sessions, containers, and local terminals, promising reduced context switching and a cleaner workflow. Adoption will depend on the quality of integration with popular shells and remote environments, as well as performance.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 主打“一个终端统治一切”的理念，将多个 shell 会话和工作流集中在同一界面。它面向需要同时管理 SSH、容器与本地终端的开发者，承诺降低上下文切换、提升工作流整洁度。是否被广泛采用取决于对主流 shell 与远程环境的集成质量以及性能表现。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>377</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48756578' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>165</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48848425" data-id="48848425" data-tags="AI" data-score="375" data-comments="483" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48848425" data-time="1783614104">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ai-2040.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI 2040: Plan A</span><span class='zh'>AI 2040：计划 A</span></a><span class="dom">ai-2040.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.howtogeek.com/why-developers-are-ditching-github-for-codeberg-and-self-hosting-alternatives/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why developers are ditching GitHub for Codeberg and self-hosting alternatives</span><span class='zh'>为何开发者弃用 GitHub 转投 Codeberg 与自托管替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">howtogeek.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A growing share of developers are moving away from GitHub toward Codeberg and other self-hosted options due to concerns about data sovereignty, privacy, and platform control.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的开发者正在离开 GitHub，转向 Codeberg 与自托管的代码托管方案，原因包括数据主权、隐私与对平台控制权的考量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A growing share of developers are moving away from GitHub toward Codeberg and other self-hosted options due to concerns about data sovereignty, privacy, and platform control. These alternatives offer open-source stacks and greater compliance flexibility, but they trade ecosystem scale and tooling maturity.</span><span class='zh'>越来越多的开发者正在离开 GitHub，转向 Codeberg 与自托管的代码托管方案，原因包括数据主权、隐私与对平台控制权的考量。这些替代方案提供开源栈和更灵活的合规性，但在生态规模和工具链的成熟度上需要权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hidden authentication backdoors exist in multiple Tenda firmware versions, potentially letting attackers bypass login.</span><span class='zh'>CERT 发现多版 Tenda 固件中存在隐藏认证后门，可能绕过登录实现远程访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hidden authentication backdoors exist in multiple Tenda firmware versions, potentially letting attackers bypass login. The finding underscores ongoing firmware and supply-chain risks in consumer networking gear, so users should update firmware and review credentials.</span><span class='zh'>CERT 发现多版 Tenda 固件中存在隐藏认证后门，可能绕过登录实现远程访问。此漏洞揭示消费级路由器固件的供应链安全风险，用户应尽快升级固件并更换默认凭据。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MIT&#x27;s 1986 SICP video lectures on programming education emphasize abstraction and recursion, shaping how software design is taught and understood.</span><span class='zh'>MIT 的 1986 年《计算机程序的结构与解释》视频讲座强调抽象与递归等核心理念，深刻影响了计算机科学的教学与软件设计思维。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MIT&#x27;s 1986 SICP video lectures on programming education emphasize abstraction and recursion, shaping how software design is taught and understood. The lectures remain a foundational reference for thinking about problem-solving and building robust software.</span><span class='zh'>MIT 的 1986 年《计算机程序的结构与解释》视频讲座强调抽象与递归等核心理念，深刻影响了计算机科学的教学与软件设计思维。至今仍被视为理解问题求解与构建健壮软件的基础参考。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flint provides a visual language to model data flows, decisions, and actions for AI agents, enabling rapid prototyping without heavy coding.</span><span class='zh'>Flint 提供一种用于 AI 代理的数据流、决策点和执行动作的可视化语言，使团队可以在不写大量代码的情况下快速原型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flint provides a visual language to model data flows, decisions, and actions for AI agents, enabling rapid prototyping without heavy coding. By graphically mapping agent behavior, teams can design, test, and debug multi-step workflows more efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>Flint 提供一种用于 AI 代理的数据流、决策点和执行动作的可视化语言，使团队可以在不写大量代码的情况下快速原型。通过图形化表示代理行为，设计、测试和调试多步骤工作流的效率将显著提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcmag.com/news/a-hackers-arrest-reveals-microsoft-can-track-users-via-a-windows-device" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft Can Track Users via a Windows Device ID</span><span class='zh'>微软可通过Windows设备ID追踪用户</span></a><span class="dom">pcmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Windows Device ID can enable persistent user tracking beyond cookies, according to a hacker&#x27;s arrest and coverage.</span><span class='zh'>一枚Windows设备ID可能在跨越Cookie之外持续跟踪用户，该结论来自一位黑客被捕的报道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Windows Device ID can enable persistent user tracking beyond cookies, according to a hacker&#x27;s arrest and coverage. The ID can survive reinstalls and be linked across apps and services, highlighting ongoing privacy trade-offs in modern Windows telemetry. The takeaway is that device identifiers remain a potent privacy concern that requires clear controls.</span><span class='zh'>一枚Windows设备ID可能在跨越Cookie之外持续跟踪用户，该结论来自一位黑客被捕的报道。该ID可在重装系统后仍然存在，并在应用与服务之间建立关联，凸显现代Windows遥测中的隐私权衡。核心启示是设备标识仍然是一个强隐私风险点，需要更清晰的控制选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg5pd9z2487o" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bonnie Tyler has died</span><span class='zh'>邦妮·泰勒去世</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Iconic vocalist Bonnie Tyler has died, marking the end of an era defined by raspy, powerful ballads that helped shape the sound of 1980s rock.</span><span class='zh'>著名歌手邦妮·泰勒去世，标志着以粗哑嗓音和强烈情感著称的80年代摇滚风格时代的告别。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Iconic vocalist Bonnie Tyler has died, marking the end of an era defined by raspy, powerful ballads that helped shape the sound of 1980s rock. Her signature tracks and unmistakable voice continue to resonate on streaming playlists and in covers, keeping her legacy alive for new generations.</span><span class='zh'>著名歌手邦妮·泰勒去世，标志着以粗哑嗓音和强烈情感著称的80年代摇滚风格时代的告别。她的代表作与独特嗓音在流媒体歌单和翻唱作品中持续回响，确保了她的音乐遗产得以传承。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.damninteresting.com/a-possible-future/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A possible future for Damn Interesting</span><span class='zh'>Damn Interesting 的未来可能走向</span></a><span class="dom">damninteresting.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article speculates on how Damn Interesting could evolve in the AI era, potentially adopting new formats and monetization models while preserving its science storytelling focus.</span><span class='zh'>本文对 Damn Interesting 在 AI 时代的未来走向进行了设想，探讨其在维持科普叙事的同时可能采用的新格式与盈利模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article speculates on how Damn Interesting could evolve in the AI era, potentially adopting new formats and monetization models while preserving its science storytelling focus. It weighs challenges like audience fragmentation and sustainable funding.</span><span class='zh'>本文对 Damn Interesting 在 AI 时代的未来走向进行了设想，探讨其在维持科普叙事的同时可能采用的新格式与盈利模式。文章也讨论了观众分化与长期资金可持续性等挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://datacenter.iers.org/data/latestVersion/bulletinC.txt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>No leap second will be introduced at the end of December 2026</span><span class='zh'>2026 年底将不再加入闰秒</span></a><span class="dom">datacenter.iers.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The International Earth Rotation and Reference Systems Service confirms no leap second will be added at the end of 2026.</span><span class='zh'>国际时刻参考系统宣布 2026 年底不再添加闰秒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The International Earth Rotation and Reference Systems Service confirms no leap second will be added at the end of 2026. This continues a trend toward simpler, predictable timekeeping for distributed systems. Engineers should plan around a leap-second-free timeline while staying alert for policy updates.</span><span class='zh'>国际时刻参考系统宣布 2026 年底不再添加闰秒。这延续了简化且可预测的全球时间计量趋势，便于分布式系统的时钟同步。工程师应在无闰秒的时间线下开展计划，并关注未来的政策变动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.zdnet.com/home-and-office/networking/spacex-wants-to-launch-100000-more-starlink-satellites/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX wants to launch 100k more Starlink satellites for 100x the bandwidth</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX计划再发射十万颗 Starlink 卫星以实现百倍带宽</span></a><span class="dom">zdnet.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SpaceX aims to deploy up to 100,000 new Starlink satellites to boost total bandwidth by around 100x.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 计划再发射十万颗 Starlink 卫星，带宽有望实现约100倍的提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SpaceX aims to deploy up to 100,000 new Starlink satellites to boost total bandwidth by around 100x. The plan would dramatically reshape global connectivity, but faces colossal challenges in propulsion, spectrum, orbital debris, and regulatory approvals. If realized, it could push internet access to remote regions while stressing space logistics and climate concerns.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 计划再发射十万颗 Starlink 卫星，带宽有望实现约100倍的提升。此举可能彻底改变全球连接格局，但在推进、频谱、轨道碎片与监管方面仍面临巨大挑战。若实现，将使偏远地区上网更易，但也对太空运营与环境提出新压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://connectsci.au/news/news-parent/9703/Spider-venom-kills-varroa-mites-without-harming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spider venom kills varroa mites without harming honeybees</span><span class='zh'>蜘蛛毒素能杀死瓦螨而不伤蜜蜂</span></a><span class="dom">connectsci.au</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spider venom has been shown to kill Varroa destructor mites without harming honeybees, offering a potential breakthrough for beekeeping.</span><span class='zh'>某种蜘蛛毒素可杀死瓦螨而不伤蜜蜂，为养蜂业带来或许的突破。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spider venom has been shown to kill Varroa destructor mites without harming honeybees, offering a potential breakthrough for beekeeping. If scalable, it could reduce colony losses and chemical miticide use, though delivery methods and safety will matter for adoption.</span><span class='zh'>某种蜘蛛毒素可杀死瓦螨而不伤蜜蜂，为养蜂业带来或许的突破。若能规模化，可能降低蜂群损失与化学杀螨剂的使用，但投放方式、特异性与安全性将决定其推广程度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A service charges $10k per week to scrub AI-generated code from repositories, addressing concerns about licensing, attribution, and ownership of machine-generated code.</span><span class='zh'>一家服务机构收取每周1万美元为代码库清除 AI 生成代码，聚焦于许可、署名与所有权等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A service charges $10k per week to scrub AI-generated code from repositories, addressing concerns about licensing, attribution, and ownership of machine-generated code. It highlights how organizations are already negotiating how to handle AI-derived assets and maintain auditable codebases.</span><span class='zh'>一家服务机构收取每周1万美元为代码库清除 AI 生成代码，聚焦于许可、署名与所有权等问题。此举反映企业在如何管理 AI 派生资产、并保持可审计代码库方面所面临的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.euronews.com/culture/2026/06/29/dua-lipa-opens-library-for-banned-and-censored-books-in-portugal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dua Lipa opens library for banned and censored books in Portugal</span><span class='zh'>杜阿·利帕在葡萄牙开设被禁书籍图书馆</span></a><span class="dom">euronews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dua Lipa opens a library in Portugal focused on banned and censored titles, spotlighting censorship issues and giving readers access to restricted titles.</span><span class='zh'>杜阿·利帕在葡萄牙开设了专门收录被禁及审查书籍的图书馆，聚焦言论自由问题并为读者提供受限书籍的获取途径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dua Lipa opens a library in Portugal focused on banned and censored titles, spotlighting censorship issues and giving readers access to restricted titles. The move blends advocacy and culture, encouraging conversations about free expression and the power of libraries as spaces for challenge and learning.</span><span class='zh'>杜阿·利帕在葡萄牙开设了专门收录被禁及审查书籍的图书馆，聚焦言论自由问题并为读者提供受限书籍的获取途径。此举将倡导与文化结合起来，推动关于自由表达的讨论，并凸显图书馆在促进挑战性与学习方面的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/30/the-father-of-the-internet-is-finally-retiring/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, “father of the Internet”, is retiring</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父 Vint Cerf 将退休</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles.</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles. His retirement signals a generational shift in Internet governance as new platforms and threats emerge. Cerf’s career underscores the enduring link between protocol design, policy, and global connectivity.</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。此举标志着互联网治理的世代转变，面对新平台与新威胁，行业将迎来新的领导力。 Cerf 的职业生涯强调了协议设计、政策制定与全球互联的持久联系。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/linuxrebel/DocuBrowser" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turning a pile of documents into a searchable useable knowledge base</span><span class='zh'>把一堆文档变成可检索的知识库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates practical methods for turning scattered documents into a searchable knowledge base, using indexing, OCR, and semantic search to unlock usable information.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示了将散落的文档转化为可检索知识库的实用方法，结合索引、OCR和语义检索来提取可用信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates practical methods for turning scattered documents into a searchable knowledge base, using indexing, OCR, and semantic search to unlock usable information. It showcases how lightweight tooling can dramatically improve personal data organization and retrieval.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示了将散落的文档转化为可检索知识库的实用方法，结合索引、OCR和语义检索来提取可用信息。此举凸显了轻量级工具在个人数据整理与检索中的显著效用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/karola3vax/CS2FOW" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War: Server-sided anti-wallhack occlusion culling for CS2 servers</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War：服务器端的遮挡剔除以对抗穿墙作弊</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War implements server-side occlusion culling to hide unseen geometry, reducing information leakage and helping prevent wallhack-style cheating.</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War 在服务器端实现遮挡剔除，隐藏玩家看不到的几何信息，降低穿墙作弊的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War implements server-side occlusion culling to hide unseen geometry, reducing information leakage and helping prevent wallhack-style cheating. The approach shifts some visibility logic to the server, potentially impacting latency and fairness.</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War 在服务器端实现遮挡剔除，隐藏玩家看不到的几何信息，降低穿墙作弊的可能性。该做法将可见性的一部分放在服务器端，可能对延迟与游戏公平性产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dicroce/wyrm_math" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Wyrm – Solve algebra by touch, built on an open-source soundness engine</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Wyrm — 通过触觉解代数，基于开源 soundness 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wyrm lets users solve algebraic problems through touch-based interaction, built on an open-source soundness engine.</span><span class='zh'>Wyrm 让用户通过触觉交互来求解代数题，底层依赖开源的 soundness 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wyrm lets users solve algebraic problems through touch-based interaction, built on an open-source soundness engine. The project demonstrates novel human-computer interaction for math education and accessibility, potentially enabling new ways to teach and explore algebraic concepts. It also provides a glimpse into how formal-method tooling can empower creative coding.</span><span class='zh'>Wyrm 让用户通过触觉交互来求解代数题，底层依赖开源的 soundness 引擎。该项目展示了用于数学教育和无障碍的新型人机交互方式，或为教学和探索代数概念带来新路径，同时体现了形式化工具在创意编码中的潜在应用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frugon is a local-first tool that analyzes prompts to determine whether a cheaper model could handle the LLM call, helping teams cut inference costs without sacrificing quality.</span><span class='zh'>Frugon 是一款本地优先的工具，能够分析提示并判断是否可由更便宜的模型处理某些 LLM 调用，从而降低推理成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frugon is a local-first tool that analyzes prompts to determine whether a cheaper model could handle the LLM call, helping teams cut inference costs without sacrificing quality. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aatishb/patterncollider" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Patterncollider: Generate and explore quasiperiodic tiling patterns</span><span class='zh'>Patterncollider：生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Patterncollider, a tool to generate and explore quasiperiodic tilings, enabling interactive study of non-repeating patterns like Penrose tilings.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Patterncollider，这一工具用于生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案，支持对非重复图案（如 Penrose 镶嵌）进行交互式研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Patterncollider, a tool to generate and explore quasiperiodic tilings, enabling interactive study of non-repeating patterns like Penrose tilings. It invites mathematical exploration and potential applications in art and game design.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Patterncollider，这一工具用于生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案，支持对非重复图案（如 Penrose 镶嵌）进行交互式研究。适用于数学爱好者，以及在艺术和游戏设计中的潜在应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Cpp2Rust/cpp2rust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust: Translates C++ to safe Rust automatically</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust：自动将 C++ 转换为安全 Rust</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust automatically translates C++ code to Rust with safety guarantees, easing migration of legacy codebases.</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust 能自动将 C++ 代码转换为具有安全性保证的 Rust，帮助遗留代码的迁移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust automatically translates C++ code to Rust with safety guarantees, easing migration of legacy codebases. While it promises safer memory management, developers should scrutinize correctness and performance post-migration and plan for integration challenges. It opens a path to safer reimplementations of old code.</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust 能自动将 C++ 代码转换为具有安全性保证的 Rust，帮助遗留代码的迁移。虽然承诺更安全的内存管理，但迁移后仍需对正确性和性能进行审查，并考虑集成中的挑战。为旧代码提供了更安全的重实现路径。</span></p>

It supports reproducible testing and auditing of AI-produced SQL.</span><span class='zh'>Sqlsure 提供对 AI 生成 SQL 的确定性语义检查，帮助确保查询在语义与安全性方面的一致性。支持对 AI 产出 SQL 进行可重复的测试与审计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/weirdGuy/kastor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Kastor – Terraform-style specs for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Kastor — 面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kastor offers Terraform-style specifications for composing AI agents, enabling declarative definitions of agent capabilities and workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Kastor 提供了面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格，使代理能力与工作流的声明性定义成为可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kastor offers Terraform-style specifications for composing AI agents, enabling declarative definitions of agent capabilities and workflows. This approach could simplify building and coordinating complex AI systems, improving reproducibility and collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Kastor 提供了面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格，使代理能力与工作流的声明性定义成为可能。此做法或将简化复杂 AI 系统的构建与协作，提升可重复性与模块化程度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bkuan001/halo-record" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Halo – open-source, tamper-evident runtime evidence for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Halo：开源、防篡改的 AI 代理运行时证据</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Halo records tamper-evident runtime evidence of AI agent actions, enabling auditing and accountability after the fact.</span><span class='zh'>Halo 能以防篡改的方式记录 AI 代理行动的运行时证据，便于事后审计与问责，是提升 AI 系统可验证溯源性的一个重要步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Halo records tamper-evident runtime evidence of AI agent actions, enabling auditing and accountability after the fact. It represents a step toward verifiable provenance in AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Halo 能以防篡改的方式记录 AI 代理行动的运行时证据，便于事后审计与问责，是提升 AI 系统可验证溯源性的一个重要步骤。</span></p>
